Moody: 150 interviewees and Life After Life 1975 read (search summaries of Cambridge history-of-term chapter, encyclopedia.com); 'introduced the term' CHANGED to 'popularised' because Lilly used it in 1972 (snippet only, Cambridge chapter); b. 30 Jun 1944 snippet only (encyclopedia.com, Wikipedia). Kurtz criticism snippet only. Ring Life at Death 1980 five stages, first peace then body separation read (Hartford encyclopedia entry, Psi Encyclopedia, snippet level). Greyson 1983 JNDS 171:369-375, 16 items 0-2, 0-32, cutoff 7 read (UVA scale description PDF and PMC studies citing it, secondary). Blanke 2002 Nature 419:269-270 sinking/falling/seeing self lying in bed from above, right angular gyrus, snippet only (EPFL PDF fetch got 429; secondary summaries); stimulation-strength mapping (weak/strong) NOT CONFIRMED in primary text. Nelson 2006 Neurology 66:1003-1009, 55 NDE vs matched controls, 60% vs 24% read (Nature news, AAN press release; abstract at uky scholars); the lesson's limit 'groups not recruited the same way' CHANGED to 'NDE group came from one research foundation's network' (Nature news says NDE subjects came via the NDE Research Foundation). Borjigin 2013 PNAS gamma surge within 30 s, nine rats, read (search summaries of PubMed/ADS/NatGeo, PubMed fetch blocked); Greyson, Kelly, Dunseath letter read at PMC3839773 (anesthetised rats lacked the pattern). Mobbs and Watt 2011 TICS 15(10):447-449 and Greyson, Holden, van Lommel 2012 TICS 16(9):445 reply read (Edinburgh pure, search summaries). Irreducible Mind 2007 Rowman and Littlefield, six authors, filter theory of Myers and James read (publisher blurb via search, UVA DOPS). ch13-origins (web, 2026-11-07): Gobekli Tepe c.9500-8000 calBC, pillars 3-5.5 m, settlement with ritual component, 2019 domestic findings, no clear agriculture in monumental complex, UNESCO 2018, name "Potbelly Hill", ~15 km from Sanliurfa (web: dainst.org/en/research/projects/noslug/5746; Wikipedia Gobekli Tepe; whc.unesco.org/en/list/1572 via search; encyclopedia.com; britannica 403 so not used); Klaus Schmidt 1953-2014, led digs from 1995/96 (web: Wikipedia, DAI blog tepe-telegrams, FAU obituary); Shanidar 4 found 1960 by Solecki, Leroi-Gourhan pollen 1975, Hunt et al. 28 Aug 2023 J Archaeol Sci bees (web: ljmu.ac.uk news 2023/8/29, livescience, johnhawks.net, Smithsonian blog); La Chapelle-aux-Saints found 1908, Rendu et al. PNAS 2014 (fieldwork 1999-2012), Dibble et al. J Archaeol Sci 2015 (web: nbcnews/NatGeo/ro.uow.edu.au, Wikipedia LCAS1); Neanderthals died out ~45,000 y ago (web: ljmu); Lion-man ~40,000 y, 31 cm, >200 fragments (Hahn 1969), Ulm Museum (web: Wikipedia Lion-man, geopark-alb.de); Chauvet ~36,000 y, >1,000 drawings, UNESCO 2014 (web: artnet, thelocal.fr, bradshawfoundation); Tylor 1832-1917, Primitive Culture 1871, minimum definition belief in spiritual beings (web: britannica.com/biography/Edward-Burnett-Tylor, britannica animism); Bird-David 1999 Current Anthropology 40 suppl, Nayaka fieldwork 1978-79 (web: journals.uchicago.edu/doi/epdf/10.1086/200061, scilit, understandingreligion.org.uk); Peoples-Duda-Marlowe 2016 Human Nature, 33 societies, 7 traits, animism oldest, active high gods 15%, afterlife then shamanism/ancestor worship (web: pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC4958132). Lion-man "mammoth ivory" only in image caption (Commons description). Numbers by arithmetic: 40000-11500=28500; 0.15*33=4.95. Ladder of three steps is the lesson's own working test (not attributed). Images: gobekli-pillar (Zhengan CC BY-SA 4.0), lion-man (Thilo Parg CC BY-SA 4.0).
